# Dependency map

Generated by `npm run depmap`. Do not edit this file by hand.

## What this is

The game source is 16 files in `src/game/`. The build joins them, in the order below, into one function. A name created in one file can be read by the others. This map is the list of those names. It is a blueprint for splitting the game into real modules without changing how it plays.

The joined function is `s8`. It starts in `src/game/00-core.js` at line 71. The React runtime wraps that function; it is not itself the shared scope. Anything in `00-core.js` above the function is a helper outside it.

## Counts

- **548 shared names** are declared in the fragments and used by more than one file.
- **3 names** are declared in the React runtime and used by more than one fragment. They stay in the runtime.
- **12 globals** (`Math`, `window`, and the like) are also used by more than one file. The game does not declare them.
- **1060 names** are declared in the fragments (the joined function, plus helpers in the wrapper that sit in a fragment). **26** distinct globals appear.
- **512 names** are touched by only one file. Those are the easy ones to move out.
- **724 cross-file calls** (4473 call sites) go from one file to a function defined in another.
- **10 load-order links.** A link means file A sets up a name that file B uses while the game is still starting. Function declarations are visible everywhere in the joined function, so a call to one is not a load-order link.
- The most coupled file is `src/game/60-frame-draw.js`.

Join order:

1. `src/game/00-core.js`
2. `src/game/10-ch1.js`
3. `src/game/11-ch1-hall.js`
4. `src/game/12-ch1-redesign.js`
5. `src/game/13-ch1-slice.js`
6. `src/game/14-ch1-day1.js`
7. `src/game/14-ch1-day2.js`
8. `src/game/15-ch1-scene1.js`
9. `src/game/20-ch2.js`
10. `src/game/25-capture.js`
11. `src/game/30-ch3.js`
12. `src/game/40-ch4.js`

14. `src/game/60-frame-draw.js`
15. `src/game/80-hud.js`
16. `src/game/90-input-shell.js`

## Load order

These links are the names one file uses, while the joined function is still starting, that an earlier file set up. Reordering two files that share a link changes the start-up. Files with no link between them can still change behavior if their top-level statements are reordered, because those statements run in join order even when they share no name. Do not reorder files from this list alone.

A function passed into another call (for example a listener) is not entered. Both sides of an if are treated as running.

| Must come first | Before | Names |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `00-core.js` | `90-input-shell.js` | `D`, `n`, `p` |
| `10-ch1.js` | `90-input-shell.js` | `CH1_CHIPS`, `aV`, `qn` |
| `11-ch1-hall.js` | `90-input-shell.js` | `B`, `le`, `qV`, `x` |
| `12-ch1-redesign.js` | `90-input-shell.js` | `ADULT_PX`, `ALIEN_ADULT`, `BALLOON_GRAB_SECONDS`, `BALL_NOTICE_RADIUS`, `BALL_NOTICE_SECONDS`, `CH1_BIN`, `CH1_CAM`, `CH1_FRAME`, `CH1_GUESTS`, `CH1_MAIL`, `CH1_OFFICE_DOOR`, `CH1_ROBOT`, `CH1_VENT`, `COOL_HAT_SECONDS`, `PHOTO_BONUS`, `QT_ADULT`, `SNACK_LOOK_SECONDS`, `SQ_ADULT`, `lockedWait` |
| `14-ch1-day1.js` | `90-input-shell.js` | `CH1_HOME_DOOR` |
| `20-ch2.js` | `90-input-shell.js` | `CH2_CHIPS`, `ch2` |
| `25-capture.js` | `90-input-shell.js` | `CAPTURES`, `CH1_CHECKPOINTS`, `CH3_CHECKPOINTS`, `CH4_CHECKPOINTS`, `LOCKUP_RESTART`, `NEVER_CAUGHT`, `NEVER_CAUGHT_TODO`, `NEVER_CAUGHT_WHY`, `SNEAKY_LABELS` |
| `30-ch3.js` | `90-input-shell.js` | `CH3_CHIPS`, `Q` |
| `40-ch4.js` | `90-input-shell.js` | `CH4_CHIPS`, `FV`, `H` |
| `80-hud.js` | `90-input-shell.js` | `METHOD_LINES` |

No name is used at start-up before it exists.

Callbacks passed at start-up and not entered: 9.
